Title: Histoire des Vaudois, divisée en trois parties (this scan: the front matter and Part I)
Creator: Jean Paul Perrin, Lionnois (pastor at Nyons in Dauphiné)
Publisher: Genève: pour Pierre & Jacques Chouët
Publication Date: 1619
Rights: Public Domain (printed at Geneva in 1619; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule)
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_i-OELQwHc4IC, file bub_gb_i-OELQwHc4IC_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: licenseurl http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/ (Public Domain Mark); contributor recorded by the host as 'unknown library'; no access-restricted-item flag.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged (no characters altered; the raw file contains 0 long-s glyphs, so no long-s normalisation was done; the OCR renders the 1619 long s as the letter f, left as scanned). Rights basis restated: a book printed at Geneva in 1619 is public domain by date.
Content note: Partial: the title page announces three parts (I the Waldenses, II the Albigenses, III their common doctrine and discipline) and the front matter lists all three, but the body of this scan holds Part I only (the dedication to Lesdiguières, then Part I Books I-II, ending with the 1545 massacre of Cabrières and Mérindol in Provence). Part I includes the articles agreed by the Waldensian ministers in 1532 (the Chanforan synod articles, numbered I-XVII in the file). Parts II and III are not in this file; the English of all three parts is in perrin_history-ancient-christians-valleys-alps_1847.txt. OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90% and found readable French with long s as f and some broken words; second witness for wording until a cleaner scan is vendored.
---
